Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The laws operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 179. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 87 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 179 + O(1/n^87). Summing over all modes and applying the laws convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
